# Prepared snapshot correspondence qualification

Source base: master `a761969668136f32ce99438e4b6a407094c9567e`, version 0.1.530,
with rendering foundation PR #439 integrated. The current ownership manifest
assigns exactly the correspondence implementation, dedicated tests, CPU benchmark
and this document to this scope. Instancing and static-geometry work use separate
branches and disjoint files. The foundation manifest and shared contracts are
unchanged. Final PR version preparation is a separate serialized operation.

## Corrected behavior

The predecessor array was already prepared on accepted snapshot changes and read
in O(1) per index. That implementation is retained. Its current-identity order
was also already prepared, but FindCurrent searched the original array even when
that array was unordered. A valid actor could render through GetInterpolatedInstance
while TryGetInterpolatedInstance returned false for the same current identity.

FindCurrent now searches that existing owner-local permutation for unique
unordered current identities and returns the original array index. Already ordered
snapshots keep the original direct binary search. No additional sorting, array,
copy or per-object predecessor search is introduced. Published arrays remain
immutable and GC-owned. SnapshotIdentityOrder and RenderWorld are unchanged.

Ambiguous current duplicate keys retain the original binary-search reachability
and tie, including its possible absence on arbitrary unordered inputs. Predecessor
searchable-view semantics and duplicate ties remain unchanged; this change does
not make previously unreachable unordered predecessor keys eligible. Reference
tests retain the old predecessor binary search and use an independent linear
reference for unique current identities, falling back to the original search only
for ambiguous current duplicates.

Generation changes cannot reuse an older generation. New sessions clear history;
stale same-session ticks and identical snapshot references are rejected before
preparation, while distinct equal-tick snapshots remain accepted. Transform,
mechanical pose and aim interpolation, VFX, ground/receiver decals and decoration
exceptions retain their established behavior. Publication/lifetimes, visibility,
Graphics, renderer, shared benchmark dispatch and workflows are unchanged.

## Functional validation

The new regression selection on the unchanged parent produced six failures and
one pass. Failures demonstrated missing unordered current identities, including
only 32 successful lookups in 4,096 repeated reversed-order queries. The correction
passes the existing and new correspondence, producer-scratch, copied combat payload
and swept-cluster selection. Dedicated renderer reference tests retain upload
order, wind/alpha behavior and legacy unordered/duplicate correspondence.

The exact implementation write set is:

- src/ForgeLine.Presentation/RenderSnapshotIndex.cs
- tests/ForgeLine.Presentation.Tests/SnapshotPublication/CorrespondenceContractTests.cs
- tests/ForgeLine.Presentation.Tests/SnapshotPublication/CorrespondenceLookupTests.cs
- benchmarks/ForgeLine.Rendering.Benchmarks/SnapshotCorrespondenceBenchmarks.cs
- docs/rendering/snapshot-publication/CorrespondenceQualification.md

The unchanged ownership validator accepts these five paths. The project-reference
validator accepts the 46-project repository graph with no cycles or forbidden
simulation/headless dependencies. A temporary local measurement caller is excluded
from that repository graph; no project or dependency is added to the PR.

## CPU method and limits

SnapshotCorrespondenceBenchmarks uses independent populated snapshots at ticks
31/32, 50 ms and session 1. Counts are 1,000/10,000/100,000; populations are Stable,
GenerationChurn (every tenth identity advances generation) and OneRemovalOneAddition
(current IDs 2..N+1). Current order is Sorted or CurrentReversed. The seeded grid
and movement use the foundation protocol at a translation-equivalent origin for
CPU-only work, with synthetic mesh/material handles and no terrain/asset loading.
All four alphas 0/.25/.5/1 and ratios 1/3/7 are measured: 216 frame cases per cohort.

Five scopes are separate: new-index capacity growth, warm correspondence rebuild,
accepted equal-tick Update without visibility, repeated per-index interpolation,
and repeated by-entity interpolation. The accepted-update control alternates two
distinct populated tick-32 snapshots; it is separate from the fixed 31/32 frame
pair. Its two order directions are primed before timing. The first diagnostic
capture exposed initial order/predecessor capacity allocations there; those raw
results remain available and are not labelled steady-state evidence.

Snapshots and their defensive copies are created outside timing. Preparation has
128 warmups; frame methods have one warmup; each scope retains eight chronological
samples. Here warm means capacity-primed, not proven tiered-JIT or thermal
steady state. Preparation scopes have 18 count/population/order cases and are reused
across their alpha/ratio views; they are not 216 independent preparation trials.
Frame times cover the complete ratio-sized batch, excluding Update and copies.
Process-local DOTNET_gcConcurrent=0 keeps allocation-counter accounting
consistent in both cohorts. There is no production GC change. Array capacity bytes
exclude the index object's header, visibility storage, snapshots and process memory;
cold allocated bytes include the new index and array headers. Large-to-small
retention behavior keeps the existing 65,536-instance trimming policy.

Machine: Intel Core Ultra 9 285HX, 24 logical processors; Windows 10.0.26300,
.NET 10.0.13, pinned SDK 10.0.402. Parent production components are the named
master source; the candidate is identified by exact component hashes in the raw
capture. Both use the same dedicated benchmark source. CPU cohorts precede the
final PR metadata increment; final version/build validation is recorded separately.

The parent fails current-identity completeness in all 108 reversed-order cases.
Its by-entity timings for those cases therefore do less work and are not a valid
speed comparison. Candidate results must retain complete hit counts and exact
per-index/by-entity checksums. Sorted and preparation results remain observations,
not a performance acceptance claim. Eight samples, no AB/BA counterbalancing and
uncontrolled clocks/background activity cannot certify a speedup, sustained cost
or the existing opt-in 15% native performance gate. No threshold is changed and
no GPU, Present latency, FPS or physical visual acceptance is inferred.

## Reproduction

Build with the pinned SDK and run the existing focused tests:

```powershell
dotnet restore ForgeLine.sln
dotnet build ForgeLine.sln --configuration Release --no-restore
dotnet test --project tests/ForgeLine.Presentation.Tests/ForgeLine.Presentation.Tests.csproj --configuration Release --no-build -- --filter-class 'ForgeLine.Presentation.Tests.SnapshotPublication.*' ForgeLine.Presentation.Tests.RenderSnapshotIndexTests ForgeLine.Presentation.Tests.RenderSubmission.SubmissionReferenceTests --parallel none
./build/Validate-ProjectReferences.ps1
python build/rendering/parallelization/ValidateOwnership.py --manifest docs/rendering/performance-foundation/Ownership.json --package SnapshotCorrespondenceQualification --diff-file artifacts/correspondence-diff.txt
```

The large BDN matrix is explicitly opt-in so ordinary shared `--filter *` CI does
not acquire 1,080 additional benchmark cases. No dispatcher change is required:

```powershell
$env:FORGELINE_SNAPSHOT_CORRESPONDENCE_BENCHMARKS = '1'
dotnet run --project benchmarks/ForgeLine.Rendering.Benchmarks/ForgeLine.Rendering.Benchmarks.csproj --configuration Release --no-build -- --filter '*SnapshotCorrespondenceBenchmarks*' --job Short --exporters BriefJSON
```

For the bounded raw capture, a temporary caller can reference that existing
benchmark project and invoke the public Capture method. The caller is measurement
tooling, not a repository project or a shared dispatcher extension:

```csharp
ForgeLine.Rendering.Benchmarks.SnapshotCorrespondenceBenchmarks.Capture(
    args[0], args[1], args[2], 8);
```

Pass output JSON path, an honest source identity and the source root containing
the four measured component paths. Keep the parent binaries/components separately,
run processes sequentially and preserve all captures, including failed or cold
diagnostic attempts. Raw hashes, results and the completed qualification below
bind the submitted implementation to its evidence.

## Completed CPU matrix

Final cohorts each contain 216 frame cases and 18 preparation cases. The parent
misses valid current IDs in every reversed-order case (108/216). The candidate
has zero missing IDs and zero X-position checksum disagreements across all 216.
Full transform/mechanical/aim behavior is qualified separately by reference tests.
All final warm preparation, accepted-update, per-index and by-entity samples
allocate zero bytes. Retained int-array capacity is identical to the parent.
Cold allocation increases by 8..8 bytes per new index for the cached ordering flag;
there is no recurring frame allocation.

Stable population, alpha 0.5, three-frame batch; lower-median p50 and nearest-rank
p95/p99. With eight samples, p95 and p99 both select the maximum sample. Values
are CPU observations rather than native rendering costs or performance approval.

| Roots | Current order | Prepare p50 / p95 / p99 (us) | Three frames by index p50 (us) | Three frames by entity p50 (us) | Retained array bytes | Cold allocated bytes |
| ---: | --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| 1000 | Sorted | 494.10 / 810.40 / 810.40 | 769.70 | 1142.70 | 4096 | 4192 |
| 1000 | CurrentReversed | 270.80 / 288.00 / 288.00 | 996.00 | 1916.10 | 12288 | 12432 |
| 10000 | Sorted | 98.60 / 109.00 / 109.00 | 3985.40 | 5541.50 | 65536 | 65632 |
| 10000 | CurrentReversed | 565.60 / 737.70 / 737.70 | 3970.10 | 6519.20 | 196608 | 196752 |
| 100000 | Sorted | 3867.50 / 4144.70 / 4144.70 | 42273.90 | 73136.70 | 400000 | 400096 |
| 100000 | CurrentReversed | 16668.20 / 16874.30 / 16874.30 | 44828.40 | 77996.60 | 1200000 | 1200144 |

No sorting/copy elimination is included: isolated attribution of sorting cost was
not established. The repair reuses the order that correspondence already prepares.
The original diagnostic captures retain the first two admission-capacity growth
events; the final paired harness primes both directions before warm timing.

The default BDN selection excludes this matrix without its explicit environment
opt-in. With opt-in, all five intended benchmark methods are discoverable through
the unchanged shared dispatcher.

## Original local validation

The original 0.1.531 source passes canonical restore and the complete Release
solution build with zero warnings and errors. All 29 selected snapshot,
correspondence, cluster and CPU-renderer reference tests pass with zero skips.
The unchanged repository Increment-PrVersion.ps1 prepares exactly one PATCH
from the actual origin/master 0.1.530; Validate-SemVer.ps1 validates the prepared
Git tree against that same master. Five implementation paths pass ownership
validation; Directory.Build.props is the separate serialized version exception.
The 46-project architecture check passes. All four original raw files and four
measured component hashes round-trip through the embedded archive verification.

The complete solution test suite, native rendering/visual acceptance and required
GitHub build-test remain separate integration gates. None is replaced by these
focused tests or advisory CPU samples. No gate, threshold or frozen contract is
modified, and no merge or release is part of this change.

## Integration synchronization

Master advanced to d1b9bcfd28bba6e318ea164ac762e3a9dfd369d4 after the
independent instancing/static-geometry qualifications. Their source/test changes
are preserved. No implementation path overlaps this correspondence change.
The repository version script prepares one PATCH from 0.1.532 to 0.1.533.
Canonical restore and full synchronized Release build pass with zero warnings
and errors. All 44 selected correspondence, snapshot, cluster, renderer-reference
and integrated instancing cases pass with zero skips on this synchronized source.
The historical CPU
cohorts and their exact component/source identities above are not relabelled.

## Lossless raw evidence

The gzip archive below stores all four original JSON byte streams as base64, each
with its own SHA-256. Initial diagnostic attempts are retained alongside the final
cohorts. This keeps evidence inside the explicitly owned document path. Decode the
base64 block, verify the gzip SHA-256, decompress JSON, then decode each file and
verify its per-file SHA-256. The raw timings are not reclassified as passing gates.

Archive SHA-256: `28f774bc0329d0d73c1978ae0dcbcae3a7054838e2e78e5120801001c0774270`
